# Closed board rows — smart-scheduler

> Rows that left `board.md` once their Status reached **DONE**, **DELIVERED**, or
> **CODE ACCEPTED**. They are copied here **verbatim** — same wording, same order,
> same table they sat in — and are never edited, re-worded, or re-statused afterwards.
>
> This file is **append-only**: each housekeeping sweep adds a new `## Swept <date>`
> section at the **bottom**. Older sections stay exactly as they were written.
>
> This is history, not a work list. Nothing here is waiting on anyone. For anything
> still in flight, read `board.md`.
>
> A byte-identical copy of the board as it stood immediately before the first sweep is
> kept at `archive/board-2026-08-31-pre-split.md`.

| TASK-491 | FE: **the shop QR page — tick several children, one Check-in, a RESULT PER CHILD** | (round item 3, Khwan; BE is TASK-490) | DONE — REVIEWED by @Sober (09-27) · **614/0**, tsc 0, build ok · 🔑 **the mixed result designed FIRST, and the headline proves it**: a success word only when **every** child is in; a mixed batch gets *"{in} of {total} checked in · {refused} need the front desk"* — **a count and an instruction, promising nothing** · 🔑 **"a 2xx is not a check-in" is BUILT, not remembered** — an ABSENT camp day comes back 2xx and is still `refused`; **a row the server did not answer for is `refused`, never assumed done** (📌 **defaulting an unknown to the bad outcome is the right way round for an act that consumes a family's credit**) · ✅ the result map is over what was **ASKED**, matched by id, so a missing row **cannot vanish from the screen** · ✅ a single tick is today's path **byte for byte**, which is also her answer to "is the compact row a second success reply?" — **she anticipated the objection in the design, not the report** · ✅ no retry-all (*"how a parent gets told 'already checked in' for a child who was fine"*) · ✅ the ceiling surfaced **before** the request · ✅ the four "nothing" cases re-asserted for the new shape | @Fern |

| TASK-535 | BE: 🔴 **the fail-closed route sweep reaches a real database again — "green with no database" has REGRESSED** (`lib/teacher-own-calendar-req097.test.ts`) | (found by @Sober while verifying TASK-534, 09-28) | DONE — REVIEWED by @Sober (09-28) · **the gate is back**: five runs by the SA — **three unreachable, two normal, 3459/0 every time**, tsc 0, 9.05 s / 273 files · 🔑 **the SA's diagnosis was WRONG and his is better: it was a TIMEOUT, not a database reach** — **the old sweep rebuilt every route per request, so its cost grew with routes × routes and crept toward the limit with every route anyone added**; the `ECONNRESET` was **a symptom of slowness**, and *"it fails deterministically with no database"* held **because with no database the guard's reads fail SLOWER, not faster** · 📌 **better news than the SA's story: nothing had crept into the file** — it was fine and getting slower, **which also explains TASK-488's original flake honestly and means TASK-504's property never actually broke; it was one timeout away from LOOKING broken** · 🔑 **the "next route" answer is complete, and the TRIPWIRE is the part that matters: it NAMES the unfaked read in the failure instead of leaving a timeout** ⇒ *the difference between a check that fails and a check that teaches* · ✅ guards'-reads pinned, built-once/signed-once (**0.20 s → 0.043 s**, coverage kept), **mutation N proves narrowing fails**, no other file regressed (established from **per-test times**, not a clean run) · 🔨 **@Sober ruled OUT `[test] timeout = 90000`:** the class is closed **by the fix, not a flag**; **a global 90 s turns every hang into a 90-second wait and hides slowness everywhere** — the next creeping sweep would have **eighteen times the room before it told us**; 🔑 **a per-test budget is a statement about that test, a global one is a statement about nothing** | @Jason |

## Swept 2026-10-02

Moved off `board.md` on 2026-10-02 (Marie housekeeping, ORDER 15.1): **11 orphan rows found ABOVE
the board title + 35 closed Tasks rows = 46 rows**. Board before the sweep: 140,891 bytes; the
byte-identical pre-sweep copy is `archive/board-2026-10-02-pre-sweep.md`. No row was reworded,
re-ordered, or re-statused.

🔴 **The 11 orphan rows are the known, unfixed "rows land above the title" defect again** — the
same one that swallowed 112 rows on 2026-09-29. All 11 are also parked verbatim in
`archive/board-2026-10-02-orphan-rows.md`, regardless of landing here, so no cell's prose exists
in only one place.

⚠️ **Four rows that READ closed were deliberately LEFT on the live board** because each carries an
outstanding obligation in its own status cell (`⏳ owed`): **TASK-551** and **TASK-552**. Under-draining is recoverable; sweeping a live obligation is not.
